- **security-hacker**: Offensive security agent for BearDrive. Attacks the hub's authorization boundaries — auth gate, per-project permissions, cross-org isolation, the /store sync proxy, uploads, share links, invites — and turns every real finding into a Go test that FAILS on the current tree. Never fixes anything, never grades. Use for a round of the security validation loop defined in .claude/security-goal.md. - **security-hunter**: Security hunter. Finds exploitable vulnerabilities in a bounded region of a codebase (mode A, dispatched by the vuln-scan skill with a region from regions.md), in a named area ad hoc (mode B, standalone), or in a diff plus what it touches (mode C, dispatched by the implement skill's review stage with a review brief path). Read-only. Emits CANDIDATE blocks (modes A and C) or a human-readable report (mode B). Candidates are unverified; a separate verifier adjudicates each. ([letsrevel/gauntlet](https://github.com/letsrevel/gauntlet/blob/HEAD/agents/security-hunter.md))
